local function geniter(f, s, c)
	if type(f) ~= "function" then
		local mt = getmetatable(f)

		if type(mt) == "table" and mt.__iter then
			f, s, c = mt.__iter(f)
		elseif type(f) == "table" then
			f, s, c = next, f, nil
		end
	end

	return function()
		local r = table.pack(f(s, c))

		if r[1] == nil then
			return false
		end

		c = r[1]

		return true, table.unpack(r, 1, r.n)
	end
end

local function v92(p18)
	if not p18 then
		return 0
	end

	local v93 = tostring(p18):gsub(",", ""):gsub("%s", ""):upper()
	local match = v93:match("([%d%.]+)")

	if not match then
		return 0
	end

	local v95 = tonumber(match) or 0

	if v93:find("T") then
		return v95 * 1e12
	end

	if v93:find("B") then
		return v95 * 1e9
	end

	if v93:find("M") then
		return v95 * 1000000
	end

	if v93:find("K") then
		return v95 * 1000
	end

	return v95
end

	if v97:find("epic") or v97:find("epico") then
		return 40
	end

	if v97:find("rare") or v97:find("raro") then
		return 25
	end

	if v97:find("uncommon") then
		return 15
	end

	return 5
end

		if player ~= LocalPlayer then
			local Character2 = player.Character
			local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2 and Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if HumanoidRootPart2 then
				local Magnitude = (HumanoidRootPart2.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

				if Magnitude < huge then
					huge = Magnitude

				end
			end
		end
	end

	return v110
end

						end

				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				selectedPrompt_:InputHoldBegin()
			end)
			task.wait(n2)
			pcall(function()
				selectedPrompt_:InputHoldEnd()
			end)
			task.wait(0.05)

			if not selectedPrompt_.Parent or not selectedPrompt_.Enabled then
				break
			end
		end

		pcall(function()
			if HoldDuration ~= nil and selectedPrompt_ and selectedPrompt_.Parent then
				selectedPrompt_.HoldDuration = HoldDuration
			end
		end)
		task.wait(0.08)
		pcall(function()
			Humanoid:UnequipTools()
		end)
		isStealing = false
	end)
end

end

		if p32 then

				return string.find(string.lower(tostring(p32.Name)), "flash", 1, true) ~= nil

			return

				v221()

local u226 = false
local t8 = {}

local function v230()
	u226 = false

	for _, v in ipairs(t8) do
		if typeof(v) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
			pcall(function()
				v:Disconnect()
			end)
		end
	end

	t8 = {}

	if u228 then
		pcall(function()
			task.cancel(u228)
		end)
		u228 = nil
	end

	pcall(function()
		local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

		if Character then
			local Humanoid = Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if Humanoid then
				Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
				Humanoid.Sit = false

				if Humanoid.Health > 0 and Humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
					pcall(function()
						Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
					end)
				end
			end

			if HumanoidRootPart then
				HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
				HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, math.min(HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 50), HumanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
			end
		end
	end)

	if dropBtn then
		dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
		dropBtn.Text = "DROP"
	end

	u229 = false
end
local function v236()
	if u226 then
		v230()

		return
	end

	if u229 then
		v230()

		return
	end

	u229 = true
	u226 = true

	local connection = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
		if not u226 then
			return
		end

		local v238, v239, v240 = ipairs(Players:GetPlayers())
		local v241 = geniter(v238, v239, v240)

		while true do
			local v242 = table.pack(v241())

			if not v242[1] then
				break
			end

			local v243 = v242[3]

			if v243 ~= LocalPlayer and v243.Character then
				local exitTo

				repeat
					local v245, v246, v247 = ipairs(v243.Character:GetChildren())
					local v248 = geniter(v245, v246, v247)
					local v249 = table.pack(v248())

					if v249[1] then
						local v250 = v249[3]

						while true do
							if v250:IsA("BasePart") then
								v250.CanCollide = false
							end

							local v251 = table.pack(v248())

							if not v251[1] then
								break
							end

							v250 = v251[3]
						end
					end

					exitTo = nil

					while true do
						local v252 = table.pack(v241())

						if v252[1] then
							v243 = v252[3]

							if v243 ~= LocalPlayer and v243.Character then
								exitTo = 1

								break
							end
						else
							break
						end
					end
				until exitTo ~= 1

				return
			end
		end
	end)

	table.insert(t8, connection)
	task.spawn(function()
		local v253 = tick() + 0.85

		while u226 and tick() < v253 do
			RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

			local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
			local HumanoidRootPart = Character and Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if HumanoidRootPart then
				local Velocity = HumanoidRootPart.Velocity

				HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Velocity * 10000 + Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
				RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

				if HumanoidRootPart.Parent then
					HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Velocity
				end

				RunService.Stepped:Wait()

				if HumanoidRootPart.Parent then
					HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Velocity + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
				end
			else
				RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
			end
		end

		v230()
	end)

	if dropBtn then
		dropBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 200, 120)
		dropBtn.Text = "DROP ✓"
	end
end

			Position = input.Position
			Position2 = Frame2.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					u268 = false
					t5.X = Frame2.Position.X.Scale
					t5.Y = Frame2.Position.Y.Scale
					pcall(saveSettings)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	Frame2.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			u268 = true
			Position = input.Position
			Position2 = Frame2.Position
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not u268 then
			return
		end

		if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			return
		end

		local v272 = input.Position - Position
		local X = CurrentCamera and CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.X or 800
		local Y = CurrentCamera and CurrentCamera.ViewportSize.Y or 600
		local v275 = Position2.X.Scale + v272.X / X
		local v276 = Position2.Y.Scale + v272.Y / Y

		Frame2.Position = UDim2.new(v275, 0, v276, 0)
		Frame.Position = UDim2.new(v275, 0, v276, 0)
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if u268 and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			u268 = false
			t5.X = Frame2.Position.X.Scale
			t5.Y = Frame2.Position.Y.Scale
			pcall(saveSettings)
		end
	end)
	TextButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if not u268 then
			v236()
		end
	end)
end

local function v306(p40)
	local Character = p40.Character

	if Character then
		local AdminESP = Character:FindFirstChild("AdminESP")

		if AdminESP then
			pcall(function()
				AdminESP:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		local AdminTag = Character:FindFirstChild("AdminTag")

		if AdminTag then
			pcall(function()
				AdminTag:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		local Head = Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

		if Head then
			local AdminTag2 = Head:FindFirstChild("AdminTag")

			if AdminTag2 then
				pcall(function()
					AdminTag2:Destroy()
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	if t11[p40] then
		pcall(function()
			t11[p40]:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	t10[p40] = nil
	t11[p40] = nil
end
local function v312(p41)
	if not p41 then
		return false
	end

	pcall(function()
		if p41:GetAttribute("AdminCommands") == true then
			return
		end
	end)

	if p41:GetAttribute("AdminCommands") == true then
		return true
	end

	if p41:GetAttribute("HasAdmin") == true then
		return true
	end

	if p41:GetAttribute("Admin") == true then
		return true
	end

	local function v313(p42)
		if not p42 then
			return false
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(p42:GetChildren()) do
			if not child:IsA("Tool") and not child:IsA("HopperBin") then
				continue
			end

			local v316 = string.lower(tostring(child.Name or ""))

			if v316:find("admin", 1, true) or v316 == "ap" or v316:find("panel", 1, true) or v316:find("comando", 1, true) or v316:find("command", 1, true) then
				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	if v313(p41:FindFirstChild("Backpack")) then
		return true
	end

	if v313(p41:FindFirstChild("StarterGear")) then
		return true
	end

	if p41.Character and v313(p41.Character) then
		return true
	end

	return false
end

createESP = function(p43, p44)
	local Character = p43.Character

	if not Character or p43 == LocalPlayer then
		return
	end

	v306(p43)

	if not p44 then
		return
	end

	local HumanoidRootPart = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or Character:FindFirstChild("Head")

	if not HumanoidRootPart then
		return
	end

	local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

	BillboardGui.Name = "AdminTag"
	BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 64)
	BillboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(3.5, 8, 0)
	BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true
	BillboardGui.Adornee = HumanoidRootPart
	BillboardGui.MaxDistance = 600
	BillboardGui.Parent = Character
	t11[p43] = BillboardGui

	local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")

	Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
	Frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	Frame.ClipsDescendants = true
	Frame.Parent = BillboardGui

	local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

	UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	UIStroke.Thickness = 2.5
	UIStroke.Parent = Frame

	ImageLabel.Name = "Owl"

	ImageLabel.ImageTransparency = 0.15

end

		Humanoid2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
		Humanoid2:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics, false)
	end)

	local function v357()
		local Character2 = LocalPlayer.Character

		if not Character2 then
			return false
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(Character2:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") then
				local v361 = child.Name:lower()

				if v361:find("carpet") or v361:find("fly") or v361:find("cloud") or v361:find("broom") or v361:find("jet") or v361:find("wing") or v361:find("hover") or v361:find("glider") or v361:find("flying") then

			end

	local function v362()
		pcall(function()
			if resetCooldown then
				return
			end

			local CurrentCamera2 = workspace.CurrentCamera

			if not CurrentCamera2 then
				return
			end

			if CurrentCamera2.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
				CurrentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
			end

	local function v365()
		local state = Humanoid2:GetState()

		return state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
	end
	local function v367()
		local now = tick()

		if now - n4 < 0.05 then
			return
		end

		n4 = now
		pcall(function()
			local attribute = LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")

			if attribute then
				if attribute - workspace:GetServerTimeNow() > 0 then
					LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow())
				end
			end
		end)

		for _, descendant in pairs(p47:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or descendant:IsA("NoCollisionConstraint") or descendant:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
				pcall(function()
					descendant:Destroy()
				end)
			else
				local v372

				if descendant:IsA("Attachment") then
					if descendant.Name == "A" or descendant.Name == "B" then
						pcall(function()
							descendant:Destroy()
						end)
					else
						if tostring(descendant.Name):find("Ragdoll") then
							pcall(function()
								descendant:Destroy()
							end)

							continue
						end

						if descendant:IsA("BodyVelocity") or descendant:IsA("BodyPosition") or descendant:IsA("BodyGyro") then
							pcall(function()
								descendant:Destroy()
							end)

							continue
						end

						if descendant:IsA("LinearVelocity") then
							v372 = descendant.Name:lower()

							if v372:find("rag") then
								pcall(function()
									descendant:Destroy()
								end)
							elseif descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
								descendant.Enabled = true
							end
						else
							if descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
								descendant.Enabled = true
							end

							continue
						end
					end
				else
					if descendant:IsA("BodyVelocity") or descendant:IsA("BodyPosition") or descendant:IsA("BodyGyro") then
						pcall(function()
							descendant:Destroy()
						end)

						continue
					end

					if descendant:IsA("LinearVelocity") then
						v372 = descendant.Name:lower()

						if v372:find("rag") then
							pcall(function()
								descendant:Destroy()
							end)
						elseif descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
							descendant.Enabled = true
						end
					else
						if descendant:IsA("Motor6D") then
							descendant.Enabled = true
						end

						continue
					end

					continue
				end

local function vampireInstaReset_()
	if u485 then
		return
	end

	local Character2 = LocalPlayer.Character

	if not Character2 or not Character2.Parent then
		return
	end

	local Humanoid2 = Character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

	if Humanoid2 then
		if not (Humanoid2.Health <= 0) then
			u485 = true
			pcall(function()
				LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("Balloon", false)
				Character2:SetAttribute("Balloon", false)
			end)

			local CurrentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
			local CFrame_
			local v492 = false

			if CurrentCamera2 then
				CFrame_ = CurrentCamera2.CFrame
				v492 = true
				pcall(function()
					CurrentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
					CurrentCamera2.CFrame = CFrame_
				end)
			end

			local HipHeight = Humanoid2.HipHeight

			if type(HipHeight) ~= "number" or HipHeight > 10 or HipHeight < 0 then
				HipHeight = 2
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				local n6 = 0

				while Character2 and Character2.Parent and Humanoid2 and Humanoid2.Health > 0 and n6 < 25 do
					pcall(function()
						Humanoid2.HipHeight = 1e30
						Humanoid2.AutoRotate = true

						local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if HumanoidRootPart2 then
							HumanoidRootPart2.CanCollide = false
						end

						for _, child in ipairs(Character2:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
								child.CanCollide = false
							end
						end

						if n6 >= 10 then
							Humanoid2.Health = 0
						end
					end)

					if v492 and CurrentCamera2 and CFrame_ then
						pcall(function()
							CurrentCamera2.CFrame = CFrame_
						end)
					end

					n6 += 1
					task.wait(0.05)
				end

				if Character2 and Character2.Parent and Humanoid2 then
					pcall(function()
						Humanoid2.HipHeight = HipHeight
						Humanoid2.PlatformStand = false
						Humanoid2.AutoRotate = true

						local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

						if HumanoidRootPart2 then
							HumanoidRootPart2.CanCollide = true
						end

						for _, child in ipairs(Character2:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("BasePart") and child.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
								child.CanCollide = true
							end
						end
					end)
				end

				task.delay(0.15, function()
					pcall(function()
						if CurrentCamera2 then
							CurrentCamera2.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom

							local Character3 = LocalPlayer.Character

							if Character3 then
								local Humanoid3 = Character3:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

								if Humanoid3 then
									CurrentCamera2.CameraSubject = Humanoid3
								end
							end
						end
					end)
					u485 = false
					pcall(function()
						_flashBlockBusy = false
					end)
				end)
			end)

			return
		end
	end
end

local function v526(p63)
	if not u345 and p63 then
		if p63.Parent then
			local v527 = tostring(p63.ActionText or ""):lower()
			local v528 = tostring(p63:GetAttribute("State") or ""):lower()

			if v527:find("lock", 1, true) or v528:find("lock", 1, true) or v527:find("unlock", 1, true) then
				return
			end

			u345 = true

			local n7 = 0.1

			pcall(function()
				if p63.HoldDuration and p63.HoldDuration > 0 then
					n7 = math.clamp(p63.HoldDuration, 0.05, 1.5)
				else
					n7 = 0.3
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				if fireproximityprompt then
					fireproximityprompt(p63, n7)
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				v522(p63, "PromptButtonHoldBegan")
			end)
			task.wait(n7)
			pcall(function()
				v522(p63, "PromptButtonHoldEnded")
				v522(p63, "Triggered")
			end)
			pcall(function()
				if fireproximityprompt and p63 and p63.Parent and p63.Enabled then
					fireproximityprompt(p63)
				end
			end)
			task.wait(0.05)
			u345 = false

			return
		end
	end
end

_G.AutoGrab = _G.AutoGrab or false

local Frame

do
	local autoSteal = {
		AutoStealEnabled = false,
		StealRadius = 55,
		StealDuration = 0.2,
		Mode = "half",
		HalfFireRange = 10,
		HalfHoldMin = 1.3,
		HalfHoldMax = 2.6,
		HalfEntryDelay = 0.3,
		Data = {},
		SelectedEntry = nil
	}
	local now
	local connection7
	local connection8
	local u535 = false

	Frame = nil

	local t12 = {
		paused = false,
		pauseEnd = 0,
		pauseDur = 20
	}
	local t13 = {
		c1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		c2 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
	}
	local t14 = {
		Bg = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 8),
		Soft = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20),
		Text = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		TextDim = Color3.fromRGB(210, 210, 210),
		TextMute = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 160)
	}

	local function v539(p64)
		if not p64 then
			return false
		end

		local PlotSign = p64:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
		local YourBase = PlotSign and PlotSign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")

		return YourBase and YourBase:IsA("BillboardGui") and YourBase.Enabled == true
	end
	local function v542(p65)
		if not p65 or not p65.Parent then
			return false
		end

		local u543 = true

		pcall(function()
			u543 = p65.Enabled
		end)

		if not u543 then
			return false
		end

		local v544 = tostring(p65:GetAttribute("State") or "")
		local v545 = tostring(p65.ActionText or "")
		local v546 = v544:lower()
		local v547 = v545:lower()

		if v547:find("lock", 1, true) or v546:find("lock", 1, true) then
			return false
		end

		if v547:find("unlock", 1, true) then
			return false
		end

		if v546 == "steal" or v546 == "grab" or v547 == "steal" or v547 == "grab" then
			return true
		end

		if not v547:find("steal", 1, true) and not v547:find("grab", 1, true) then
			if not v547:find("robar", 1, true) then
				return false
			end
		end

		return true
	end
	local function n175PauseAutoGrab()
		if autoSteal.AutoStealEnabled or _G.AutoGrab then
			t12.paused = true
			t12.pauseEnd = tick() + t12.pauseDur

			if Frame then
				Frame.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end
	local function n175ResumeAutoGrab()
		t12.paused = false

		if Frame then
			Frame.Visible = false
		end
	end

	_G._175_PauseAutoGrab = n175PauseAutoGrab
	_G._175_ResumeAutoGrab = n175ResumeAutoGrab

	local function v550()
		local Plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

		if not Plots then
			return false
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Model") and not v539(child) then
				local AnimalPodiums = child:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")

				if not AnimalPodiums then
					continue
				end

				for _, child2 in ipairs(AnimalPodiums:GetChildren()) do
					local Base = child2:FindFirstChild("Base")
					local Spawn = Base and Base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
					local PromptAttachment = Spawn and Spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")

					if PromptAttachment then
						for _, child3 in ipairs(PromptAttachment:GetChildren()) do
							if child3:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v542(child3) then
								return true
							end
						end
					end

					if Spawn then
						for _, descendant in ipairs(Spawn:GetDescendants()) do
							if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v542(descendant) then
								return true
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return false
	end
	local function v564(p66, p67)
		if not p66 or not p67 then
			return nil
		end

		local AnimalPodiums = p66:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")

		if not AnimalPodiums then
			return nil
		end

		local v566 = AnimalPodiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(p67))

		if not v566 then
			return nil
		end

		local Base = v566:FindFirstChild("Base")

		if Base then
			local Spawn = Base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")

			if Spawn then
				local PromptAttachment = Spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")

				if PromptAttachment then
					for _, child in ipairs(PromptAttachment:GetChildren()) do
						if child:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v542(child) then
							return child
						end
					end
				end

				for _, descendant in ipairs(Spawn:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v542(descendant) then
						return descendant
					end
				end
			end

			for _, descendant in ipairs(Base:GetDescendants()) do
				if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v542(descendant) then
					return descendant
				end
			end
		end

		for _, descendant in ipairs(v566:GetDescendants()) do
			if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v542(descendant) then
				return descendant
			end
		end

		return nil
	end
	local function v578()
		if autoSteal.SelectedEntry and autoSteal.SelectedEntry.prompt then
			local prompt2 = autoSteal.SelectedEntry.prompt

			if prompt2 and prompt2.Parent and prompt2:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v542(prompt2) then
				local plot = autoSteal.SelectedEntry.plot

				if plot and plot.Parent and not v539(plot) then
					return prompt2
				end
			end

			autoSteal.SelectedEntry = nil
		end

		local Character2 = LocalPlayer.Character

		if not Character2 then
			return nil
		end

		local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or Character2:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or Character2:FindFirstChild("Torso")

		if not HumanoidRootPart2 then
			return nil
		end

		if prompt and prompt.Parent and v542(prompt) then
			local WorldPosition

			pcall(function()
				local Parent = prompt.Parent

				if Parent and Parent:IsA("Attachment") then
					WorldPosition = Parent.WorldPosition
				elseif Parent and Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
					WorldPosition = Parent.Position
				end
			end)

			if WorldPosition and (HumanoidRootPart2.Position - WorldPosition).Magnitude <= autoSteal.StealRadius then
				return prompt
			end
		end

		local Plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

		if not Plots then
			return nil
		end

		local v586
		local huge = math.huge

		for _, child in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Model") and not v539(child) then
				local AnimalPodiums = child:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")

				if AnimalPodiums then
					for _, child2 in ipairs(AnimalPodiums:GetChildren()) do
						local Base = child2:FindFirstChild("Base")
						local Spawn = Base and Base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")

						if Spawn then
							local Magnitude = (Spawn.Position - HumanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude

							if Magnitude <= autoSteal.StealRadius and Magnitude < huge then
								local v596 = v564(child, tonumber(child2.Name) or child2.Name)

								if v596 then
									v586 = v596
									huge = Magnitude
								else
									for _, descendant in ipairs(Spawn:GetDescendants()) do
										if descendant:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and v542(descendant) then
											v586 = descendant
											huge = Magnitude

											break
										end
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return v586
	end
	local function v599(p68)
		local Character2 = LocalPlayer.Character

		if not Character2 then
			return math.huge
		end

		local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or Character2:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or Character2:FindFirstChild("Torso")

		if not HumanoidRootPart2 then
			return math.huge
		end

		local Parent = p68.Parent

		if Parent and Parent:IsA("Attachment") then
			Parent = Parent.Parent
		end

		if Parent and Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
			return (Parent.Position - HumanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude
		end

		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			if p68.Parent then
				if p68.Parent:IsA("Attachment") then
					return p68.Parent.WorldPosition
				end
			end

			return nil
		end)

		if ok and result then
			return (result - HumanoidRootPart2.Position).Magnitude
		end

		return math.huge
	end
	local function v605(p69)
		if u345 or t12.paused or u344 then
			return
		end

		if not p69 or not p69.Parent or not v542(p69) then
			return
		end

		if Frame and _G._175_FlashActive then
			pcall(function()
				if _G._175_AutoGrabScreenGui then
					_G._175_AutoGrabScreenGui.Enabled = true
				end
			end)
			Frame.Visible = true
		end

		if not autoSteal.Data[p69] then
			autoSteal.Data[p69] = {
				hold = {},
				trigger = {},
				ready = true
			}

			if getconnections then
				pcall(function()
					for _, v in ipairs(getconnections(p69.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
						if v.Function then
							table.insert(autoSteal.Data[p69].hold, v.Function)
						end
					end

					for _, v in ipairs(getconnections(p69.Triggered)) do
						if v.Function then
							table.insert(autoSteal.Data[p69].trigger, v.Function)
						end
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		local v610 = autoSteal.Data[p69]

		if not v610.ready then
			return
		end

		v610.ready = false
		u345 = true
		now = tick()

		if autoSteal.Mode == "half" then
			task.spawn(function()
				for _, v in ipairs(v610.hold) do
					task.spawn(v)
				end

				pcall(function()
					if fireproximityprompt then
						fireproximityprompt(p69, autoSteal.HalfHoldMin)
					end
				end)
				pcall(function()
					if p69.InputHoldBegin then
						p69:InputHoldBegin()
					end
				end)
				task.wait(autoSteal.HalfHoldMin)

				local v613 = v599(p69) <= autoSteal.HalfFireRange

				while not (autoSteal.HalfHoldMax < tick() - now) and p69.Parent do
					if v599(p69) <= autoSteal.HalfFireRange then
						if not v613 then
							task.wait(autoSteal.HalfEntryDelay)
						end

						for _, v in ipairs(v610.trigger) do
							task.spawn(v)
						end

						pcall(function()
							if fireproximityprompt then
								fireproximityprompt(p69)
							end
						end)
						pcall(function()
							if p69.InputHoldEnd then
								p69:InputHoldEnd()
							end
						end)
						task.wait(0.05)
						v610.ready = true
						u345 = false

						return
					end

					task.wait()
				end

				task.wait(0.05)
				v610.ready = true
				u345 = false
			end)
		else
			task.spawn(function()
				for _, v in ipairs(v610.hold) do
					task.spawn(v)
				end

				pcall(function()
					if fireproximityprompt then
						fireproximityprompt(p69, autoSteal.StealDuration)
					end
				end)
				pcall(function()
					if p69.InputHoldBegin then
						p69:InputHoldBegin()
					end
				end)

				local n8 = 0

				while n8 < autoSteal.StealDuration do
					n8 += task.wait()
				end

				for _, v in ipairs(v610.trigger) do
					task.spawn(v)
				end

				pcall(function()
					if fireproximityprompt then
						fireproximityprompt(p69)
					end
				end)
				pcall(function()
					if p69.InputHoldEnd then
						p69:InputHoldEnd()
					end
				end)
				task.wait(0.05)
				v610.ready = true
				u345 = false
			end)
		end
	end
	local function v621()
		if connection7 then
			return
		end

		autoSteal.AutoStealEnabled = true
		_G.AutoGrab = true
		t12.paused = false
		connection7 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()

			end
		end)

		end

		print("[175] Auto Grab ENABLED (panel + half mode)")
	end
	local function v623()

		_G.AutoGrab = false
		t12.paused = false

		end

		if Frame then

		end

	end
	local function v624()
		if connection8 then
			return
		end

		connection8 = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if u32 then
				pcall(function()
					connection8:Disconnect()
				end)
				connection8 = nil

				return
			end

			if t12.paused and t12.pauseEnd <= tick() then
				n175ResumeAutoGrab()
			end

			local v625 = v550()

			if v625 and not u535 then
				u535 = true

				if Frame then
					Frame.Visible = false
				end

				if not autoSteal.AutoStealEnabled and _G.AutoGrab and not t12.paused then
					v621()
				end
			elseif not v625 and u535 then
				u535 = false

				if Frame then
					Frame.Visible = false
				end
			end
		end)
		table.insert(t1, connection8)
	end
	local function v626()
		if connection8 then
			pcall(function()
				connection8:Disconnect()
			end)
			connection8 = nil
		end
	end
	local function v627()
		local StealProgressScreenGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("StealProgressScreenGui")

		local Position
		local Position2
		local u644 = false

		Frame5.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)

				u641 = true
				Position = input.Position
				Position2 = Frame.Position
				u644 = false
			end
		end)
		Frame5.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				u641 = false
			elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				u641 = false

				return
			end
		end)
		UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if not u641 then
				return
			end

			if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				return
			end

			local v645 = input.Position - Position

			if not u644 then
				if v645.Magnitude < 3 then
					return
				end

			end

			Frame.Position = UDim2.new(Position2.X.Scale, Position2.X.Offset + v645.X, Position2.Y.Scale, Position2.Y.Offset + v645.Y)
		end)

		local n9 = 0
		local n10 = 0
		local s2 = ""
		local u649 = false

		RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			local visible = autoSteal.AutoStealEnabled and not t12.paused or u535

			if Frame then
				Frame.Visible = visible
			end

			if not visible then
				return
			end

				s2 = v651.ObjectText ~= "" and v651.ObjectText or "Brainrot"
			else
				u649 = false
			end

			if u345 and now then
				n11 = math.clamp((tick() - now) / 1.3, 0, 1)
			elseif u649 then
				n11 = 1
			end

			n9 += (n11 - n9) * math.clamp(dt * 14, 0, 1)
			n10 += dt

			if n10 < 0.033333333333333333 then
				return
			end

			n10 = 0

			local v653 = math.clamp(n9, 0, 1)

			Frame4.Size = UDim2.new(v653, 0, 1, 0)

			if u649 then
				TextLabel.Text = s2 ~= "" and s2 or "Stealing..."
				TextLabel2.Text = string.format("%d%%", math.floor(v653 * 100))
				TextLabel2.TextColor3 = t13.c1
			else
				TextLabel.Text = "AUTO GRAB"

				if u345 and _G._175_FlashActive then
					TextLabel2.Text = "grabbing..."
					TextLabel2.TextColor3 = t14.Text
					pcall(function()
						if _G._175_AutoGrabScreenGui then
							_G._175_AutoGrabScreenGui.Enabled = true
						end
					end)
					Frame.Visible = true
				else
					Frame.Visible = false
					pcall(function()
						if _G._175_AutoGrabScreenGui then
							_G._175_AutoGrabScreenGui.Enabled = false
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	v627()
	_G.AutoSteal = autoSteal

	local function n175StartAutoGrab()
		_G.AutoGrab = true

	end
	local function n175StopAutoGrab()

	local function v666(p70)
		if not Character then
			return nil
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") then
				if child.Name:lower():find(p70:lower()) then
					return child
				end
			end
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("Tool") then
				if child.Name:lower():find(p70:lower()) then
					return child
				end
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function v787()
		local Plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

		if not Plots then
			return
		end

		for _, child in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
			if not v699(child) and v710(child) then
				local Purchases = child:FindFirstChild("Purchases")
				local PlotBlock = Purchases and Purchases:FindFirstChild("PlotBlock")
				local Main = PlotBlock and PlotBlock:FindFirstChild("Main")
				local v794 = t17[child.Name]
				local RemainingTime = Main and Main:FindFirstChild("BillboardGui") and Main.BillboardGui:FindFirstChild("RemainingTime")

				if RemainingTime and Main then
					local TextLabel = (v794 or createBillboardGui(child, Main)):FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")

					if TextLabel then
						TextLabel.Text = RemainingTime.Text
						TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					end
				elseif v794 then
					pcall(function()
						v794:Destroy()
					end)
					t17[child.Name] = nil
				end
			elseif t17[child.Name] then
				pcall(function()
					t17[child.Name]:Destroy()
				end)
				t17[child.Name] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	local function v799(p77)
		if not p77 or not p77.Parent or not p77.Enabled then
			return false
		end

		local attribute = p77:GetAttribute("State")
		local ActionText = p77.ActionText

		if attribute == "Steal" or attribute == "Grab" or ActionText == "Steal" or ActionText == "Grab" then
			return true
		end

		return false
	end

	n175StartTripToPetSlot = function(p78, p79)
		local Character2 = LocalPlayer.Character

		if Character2 then
			Character = Character2
			Humanoid = Character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or Humanoid
			HumanoidRootPart = Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or HumanoidRootPart
		end

		if p78 then
			if p78.Parent then
				local v803 = tonumber(p79) or 1
				local v804 = t15[v803] or t15[1]

				if not v804 then
					warn("[175] Flash: slot sin config", v803)
					v804 = t15[1]
				end

				if not v804 then
					return
				end

				local Positions = v804.Positions or { v804.Position }

				if type(Positions) ~= "table" or #Positions == 0 then
					warn("[175] Flash: sin posiciones para slot", v803)

					return
				end

				local v806 = v804.NeedJump == true

				if v803 >= 19 and v803 <= 27 then
					v806 = true
				end

				if connection then
					pcall(function()
						connection:Disconnect()
					end)
					connection = nil
				end

				u344 = false
				u345 = false

				if not HumanoidRootPart or not Humanoid or not HumanoidRootPart.Parent then
					warn("[175] Flash: sin Root/Humanoid — espera a que cargue el personaje")

					return
				end

				u344 = true
				_G._175_FlashActive = true
				_G._175_ManualFlashArmed = true

				if type(_G._175_PauseAutoGrab) == "function" then
					pcall(_G._175_PauseAutoGrab)
				end

				if type(_G._175_StartFlashLagger) == "function" then
					pcall(_G._175_StartFlashLagger)
				end

				local JumpPower = Humanoid.JumpPower

				Humanoid.JumpPower = 0

							v:Stop(0)

						pcall(function()
							p80:Stop(0)
						end)

		_G._175_StartFlashCooldown = function(p90)
			local v994 = math.max(1, tonumber(p90) or 20)

			_G._175_FlashSpent = false
			n15 = v994

			local v995 = (n16 or 0) + 1

			n16 = v995
			pcall(function()
				if type(stopFlashBlink) == "function" then
					stopFlashBlink()
				end
			end)

			local v996 = v993()

			if v996 then
				v996.Text = tostring(v994) .. "s"
				v996.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			end

			pcall(function()
				if flashTp then
					flashTp.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
				end

				if v989 then
					v989.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
				end
			end)
			print("[175] Flash gastado → contador", v994, "s")
			task.spawn(function()
				while n15 > 0 and not u32 and n16 == v995 do
					local v997 = math.ceil(n15)
					local v998 = v993()

					if v998 then
						v998.Text = tostring(v997) .. "s"
						v998.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					end

					pcall(function()
						if flashTp then
							flashTp.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
						end

						if v989 then
							v989.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
						end
					end)
					task.wait(0.25)
					n15 -= 0.25
				end

				if n16 ~= v995 then
					return
				end

				n15 = 0

				local v999 = v993()

				if v999 then
					v999.Text = "FLASH [F]"
					v999.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				end

				pcall(function()
					if flashTp then
						flashTp.BackgroundColor3 = t24.card
					end

					if v989 then
						v989.BackgroundColor3 = t24.stroke or t24.accent
					end
				end)
				pcall(function()
					if type(startFlashBlink) == "function" then
						startFlashBlink()
					end
				end)
				print("[175] Flash LISTO")
			end)
		end
		_G._175_CancelFlashCooldown = function()
			n16 = (n16 or 0) + 1
			n15 = 0
			_G._175_FlashSpent = false

			local Parent = TextLabel2 and TextLabel2.Parent and TextLabel2 or flashTp and flashTp:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel")

			if Parent then
				Parent.Text = "FLASH [F]"
			end

			pcall(function()
				flashTp.BackgroundColor3 = t24.card

				if v989 then
					v989.BackgroundColor3 = t24.stroke
				end
			end)
			pcall(startFlashBlink)
		end

			local t29 = {}

			local function n175PlayUrlSound(p142, p143, p144)
				task.spawn(function()
					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
						local v1198 = t29[p142]

						if not v1198 or v1198 == "" then
							local v1199 = #p142 % 97
							local v1200 = "175_snd_" .. tostring(math.abs(string.len(p142) * 31 + v1199)) .. ".mp3"
							local n18 = 0

							for i = 1, #p142 do
								n18 = (n18 * 31 + string.byte(p142, i)) % 100000000
							end

							local v1203 = "175_snd_" .. tostring(n18) .. ".mp3"
							local Body
							local request_ = syn and syn.request or http and http.request or http_request or request

							if request_ then
								local v1206 = request_({
									Url = p142,
									Method = "GET",
									Headers = {
										["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0",
										Accept = "*/*"
									}
								})

								if v1206 then
									Body = v1206.Body or v1206.body or v1206.Data
								end
							end

							if not Body or #tostring(Body) < 100 then
								pcall(function()
									Body = game:HttpGet(p142)
								end)
							end

							if not Body or #tostring(Body) < 100 then
								warn("[175] No se pudo descargar sonido:", p142)

								return
							end

							if not writefile then
								warn("[175] writefile no disponible")

								return
							end

							writefile(v1203, Body)

							if not getcustomasset then
								warn("[175] getcustomasset no disponible")

								return
							end

							v1198 = getcustomasset(v1203)
							t29[p142] = v1198
						end

						local Sound = Instance.new("Sound")

						Sound.Name = "175_NotifySound"
						Sound.SoundId = v1198
						Sound.Volume = p143 or 1
						Sound.Looped = false
						Sound.Parent = SoundService
						Sound:Play()

						if not Sound.IsLoaded then
							pcall(function()
								Sound.Loaded:Wait()
							end)
							pcall(function()
								Sound:Play()
							end)
						end

						task.delay(p144 or 6, function()
							pcall(function()
								Sound:Stop()
								Sound:Destroy()
							end)
						end)
					end)

					if not ok then
						warn("[175] playUrlSound error:", result)
					end
				end)
			end

			_G._175_PlayUrlSound = n175PlayUrlSound

			local thread

			_G.LaggerPower = _G.LaggerPower or 50

			local function n175StartFlashLagger()
				if not _G.LaggerOnFlash then
					return
				end

				local incomingReplicationLag = math.clamp((_G.LaggerPower or 50) / 40, 0.3, 3)

				pcall(function()
					settings().Network.IncomingReplicationLag = incomingReplicationLag
				end)

				if thread then
					pcall(function()
						task.cancel(thread)
					end)
					thread = nil
				end

				thread = task.delay(2.5, function()
					pcall(function()
						settings().Network.IncomingReplicationLag = 0
					end)
					thread = nil
				end)
			end
			local function n175StopFlashLagger()
				if thread then
					pcall(function()
						task.cancel(thread)
					end)
					thread = nil
				end

				pcall(function()
					settings().Network.IncomingReplicationLag = 0
				end)
			end

			_G._175_StartFlashLagger = n175StartFlashLagger
			_G._175_StopFlashLagger = n175StopFlashLagger

task.spawn(function()
	local chunk = loadstring([[local autoTurretEnabled = _G.AutoTurretEnabled == true
local lp = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local turretConns = {}
local turretLoopRunning = false
local turretAttackBusy, turretAttackQueued, turretAttackCD = {}, {}, {}
local turretAttackActive = false
local RETRY_DELAY = 0.3
local function isEnemyTurret(obj)
    if not obj or not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
    local ownerId = obj.Name:match("^Sentry_(%d+)$")
    return ownerId ~= nil and ownerId ~= tostring(lp.UserId)
end
local function setTurretNoClip(turret)
    if isEnemyTurret(turret) then pcall(function() turret.CanCollide = false end) end
end
local function getTurretTimeLabel(turret)
    local sf = turret and turret:FindFirstChild("SetupFrame")
    local mf = sf and sf:FindFirstChild("MainFrame")
    local lbl = mf and mf:FindFirstChild("Time")
    return (lbl and lbl:IsA("TextLabel")) and lbl or nil
end
local function shouldAttackTurret(turret)
    if not lp or lp:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= nil then return false end
    if not isEnemyTurret(turret) then return false end
    setTurretNoClip(turret)
    local lbl = getTurretTimeLabel(turret)
    if not lbl then return false end
    local ok, text = pcall(function() return lbl.Text end)
    if not ok then return false end
    text = tostring(text or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    return text ~= "" and string.find(text, "^%d+s!$") ~= nil
end
local function bringTurretInFront(turret, hrp)
    if not turret or not hrp then return end
    local fwd = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
    local pos = hrp.Position + fwd * 4 + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
    pcall(function()
        if hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity ~= nil then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
        turret.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos + fwd)
    end)
end
local function attackTurret(turret)
    local now = tick()
    if turretAttackBusy[turret] or turretAttackQueued[turret] or turretAttackActive then return end
    if not shouldAttackTurret(turret) then return end
    if (turretAttackCD[turret] or 0) > now then return end
    turretAttackQueued[turret] = true
    turretAttackCD[turret] = now + RETRY_DELAY
    task.spawn(function()
        turretAttackQueued[turret] = nil
        if turretAttackActive or turretAttackBusy[turret] then return end
        if not shouldAttackTurret(turret) then return end
        turretAttackActive = true
        turretAttackBusy[turret] = true
        pcall(function()
            local attempts = 0
            while attempts < 12 and autoTurretEnabled do
                if not turret or not turret.Parent or not shouldAttackTurret(turret) then break end
                local char = lp.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then break end
                local okD, dist = pcall(function() return (turret.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude end)
                if okD and dist > 220 then break end
                setTurretNoClip(turret)
                bringTurretInFront(turret, hrp)
                local bp = lp:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                local bat = char:FindFirstChild("Bat") or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild("Bat"))
                if bat and bat.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
                bat = char:FindFirstChild("Bat") or bat
                if bat then pcall(function() bat:Activate() end) end
                task.wait(0.03)
                attempts = attempts + 1
                task.wait(0.09)
            end
        end)
        turretAttackBusy[turret] = nil
        turretAttackActive = false
    end)
end
local function disconnectAll()
    for i = 1, #turretConns do pcall(function() turretConns[i]:Disconnect() end) end
    turretConns = {}
end
local function startAutoTurret()
    disconnectAll()
    table.insert(turretConns, Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if isEnemyTurret(obj) then setTurretNoClip(obj) end
        if autoTurretEnabled and shouldAttackTurret(obj) then
            task.spawn(function() attackTurret(obj) end)
        end
    end))
    if not turretLoopRunning then
        turretLoopRunning = true
        task.spawn(function()
            while autoTurretEnabled do
                task.wait(0.4)
                for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                    if isEnemyTurret(obj) then setTurretNoClip(obj) end
                    if autoTurretEnabled and shouldAttackTurret(obj) then attackTurret(obj) end
                end
            end
            turretLoopRunning = false
        end)
    end
end
_G._175_AT = function(state)
    autoTurretEnabled = state and true or false
    _G.AutoTurretEnabled = autoTurretEnabled
    if autoTurretEnabled then startAutoTurret() else disconnectAll() end
end
if _G.AutoTurretEnabled == true then _G._175_AT(true) end
print("[175] AutoTurret ready")
]])

	if chunk then
		pcall(chunk)
	end
end)

	local function v1572()

		if not Plots then
			return false

			local PlotSign = child:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")

					continue
				end

				return true
			end
		end

		return false
	end

					return true, "unequipped"
				end

				if u1951 and u1951 ~= p231 then
					u1951 = nil
				end

				if v1935(p231) then
					u1951 = p231

					return true, "equipped"
				end

				return false, "fail"
			end

			local v1964 = n175GetVerityCoins()

			if v1964 < v1963 then
				return false, "poor"
			end

			n175SetVerityCoins(v1964 - v1963)
			t69[p231] = true
			v1934()
			pcall(function()
				if type(_G._175_SpendFx) == "function" then
					_G._175_SpendFx(v1963)
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				if type(sendShopPurchaseWebhook) == "function" then
					sendShopPurchaseWebhook(p231, v1963, "Animation")
				elseif type(_G._175_SendShopWebhook) == "function" then
					_G._175_SendShopWebhook(p231, v1963, "Animation")
				end
			end)

			if u1951 and u1951 ~= p231 then
				u1951 = nil
			end

			if v1935(p231) then
				u1951 = p231
			end

			return true, "bought"
		end

		local function v2021(p240, p241)
			local v2022 = p241 or 60

					connection11:Disconnect()

			end

				u2017 = false
				_G._175_InfJump = false

				return
			end

			_G._175_InfJump = true
			n34 = tick() + v2022
			connection11 = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
				if not u2017 or n34 < tick() then
					u2017 = false
					_G._175_InfJump = false

					if connection11 then
						pcall(function()
							connection11:Disconnect()
						end)
						connection11 = nil
					end

					return
				end

				local now = tick()

				if now - n35 < 0.18 then
					return
				end

				n35 = now

				local Character2 = LocalPlayer.Character
				local HumanoidRootPart2 = Character2 and Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				local Humanoid2 = Character2 and Character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if not HumanoidRootPart2 or not Humanoid2 then
					return
				end

				if HumanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y < 35 then
					pcall(function()
						HumanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(HumanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 50, HumanoidRootPart2.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
					end)
				end
			end)
			task.delay(v2022 + 0.05, function()
				if n34 - 0.1 <= tick() then
					u2017 = false
					_G._175_InfJump = false

					if connection11 then
						pcall(function()
							connection11:Disconnect()
						end)
						connection11 = nil
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		local function v2030(p242)
			if not p242 or not p242:IsA("Tool") then
				return false
			end

			local v2031 = string.lower(tostring(p242.Name or ""))
			local s5 = ""

			pcall(function()
				s5 = string.lower(tostring(p242.ToolTip or ""))
			end)

			for _, v in ipairs({
				"carpet",
				"flying",
				"alfombra",
				"fly",
				"magic carpet",
				"flycarpet",
				"rug",
				"mat",
				"broom"
			}) do
				if not v2031:find(v, 1, true) and not s5:find(v, 1, true) then
					continue
				end

				return true
			end

			return false
		end
		local function v2035(p243)
			if not p243 then
				return nil
			end

			for _, child in ipairs(p243:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("Tool") and v2030(child) then
					return child
				end
			end

			if p243:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
				for _, child in ipairs(p243:GetChildren()) do
					if child:IsA("Tool") then
						if v2030(child) then
							return child
						end

						local v2040 = string.lower(child.Name)

						if v2040:find("carpet") or v2040:find("fly") then
							return child
						end
					end
				end
			end

			return nil
		end

		local function v2066()
			if u2064 then
				return
			end

			u2064 = true
			task.spawn(function()
				for _, v in ipairs(t94) do
					pcall(function()
						if not isfile(v.archivo) then
							writefile(v.archivo, game:HttpGet(v.url))
						end

						local Sound = Instance.new("Sound")

						Sound.Name = "175_Music_" .. tostring(v.id)
						Sound.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
						Sound.SoundId = getcustomasset(v.archivo)
						Sound.Volume = volume
						Sound.Looped = false
						Sound.Ended:Connect(function()
							if id == v.id and u2063 then
								local v2070 = id % #t94 + 1

								pcall(function()
									_G._175_PlayMusicTrack(v2070)
								end)
							end
						end)
						v.sound = Sound
					end)
					task.wait()
				end

				print("[175] Music: " .. #t94 .. " tracks loaded")
			end)
		end
		local function v2071(p246)
			for _, v in ipairs(t95) do
				if v.cancion == p246 then
					if p246.encendida then
						v.switchBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 160, 40)
						v.knob.Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, -3)
						v.label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 255, 180)
					else
						v.switchBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
						v.knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -3)
						v.label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
					end

					break
				end
			end
		end

		_G._175_PlayMusicTrack = function(p247)
			v2066()

			for _, v in ipairs(t94) do
				if v.encendida then
					v.encendida = false

					if v.sound then
						pcall(function()
							v.sound:Stop()
						end)
					end

					v2071(v)
				end
			end

			for _, v in ipairs(t94) do
				if v.id == p247 then
					v.encendida = true
					id = v.id
					sound = v.sound
					u2062 = false

					if sound then
						sound.Volume = volume
						pcall(function()
							sound:Play()
						end)
					end

					v2071(v)

					break
				end
			end
		end

		local function v2278(p254)

				return nil

			end

			if p255 >= 1e12 then
				return "$" .. v2282(string.format("%.2f", p255 / 1e12)) .. "T/s"
			end

			if p255 >= 1e9 then
				return "$" .. v2282(string.format("%.2f", p255 / 1e9)) .. "B/s"
			end

			if p255 >= 1000000 then
				return "$" .. v2282(string.format("%.2f", p255 / 1000000)) .. "M/s"
			end

			if not (p255 >= 1000) then
				return "$" .. tostring(math.floor(p255)) .. "/s"
			end

			return "$" .. v2282(string.format("%.2f", p255 / 1000)) .. "K/s"
		end

					if v294 then
						v294()
					end
				end)
			elseif v2740(KeyCode, "ResetBalloon") and v2738("ResetBalloon") then
				_G.AutoResetOnBalloon = not _G.AutoResetOnBalloon
				pcall(function()
					if v294 then
						v294()
					end
				end)
			elseif v2740(KeyCode, "AutoBlock") and v2738("AutoBlock") then
				_G.AutoBlock = not _G.AutoBlock
				pcall(function()
					if v294 then
						v294()
					end
				end)
			elseif v2740(KeyCode, "APESP") and v2738("APESP") then
				_G.APESPEnabled = not _G.APESPEnabled
				pcall(function()
					if _G.APESPEnabled and v327 then
						v327()
					elseif v330 then
						v330()
					end

					if v294 then
						v294()
					end
				end)
			elseif v2740(KeyCode, "StealIntro") and v2738("StealIntro") then
				_G.StealIntroEnabled = _G.StealIntroEnabled == false
				pcall(function()
					if v294 then
						v294()
					end
				end)
			elseif v2740(KeyCode, "AutoReturn") and v2738("AutoReturn") then
				_G.AutoReturnBase = not _G.AutoReturnBase
				pcall(function()
					if v294 then
						v294()
					end
				end)
			elseif v2740(KeyCode, "AutoBalloon") and v2738("AutoBalloon") then
				_G.AutoBalloon = not _G.AutoBalloon
				pcall(function()
					if v294 then
						v294()
					end
				end)
			elseif v2740(KeyCode, "BackpackESP") and v2738("BackpackESP") then
				_G.BackpackESP = not _G.BackpackESP
				pcall(function()
					if v294 then
						v294()
					end
				end)
			end
		end)

		local ScreenGui

		_G._175_OpenKeybindEditor = function()
			if ScreenGui and ScreenGui.Parent then
				ScreenGui:Destroy()
				ScreenGui = nil

				return
			end

			local hui = PlayerGui

			pcall(function()
				if gethui then
					hui = gethui()
				end
			end)
			ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
			ScreenGui.Name = "175_KeybindEditor"
			ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
			ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
			ScreenGui.DisplayOrder = 140
			ScreenGui.Parent = hui

			local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame")

			Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 260, 0, 340)
			Frame2.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -130, 0.5, -170)
			Frame2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
			Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(16, 12, 14)
			Frame2.BorderSizePixel = 0
			Frame2.Active = true
			Frame2.Parent = ScreenGui
			Instance.new("UICorner", Frame2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

			local UIStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")

			UIStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 100)
			UIStroke.Thickness = 1.2
			UIStroke.Parent = Frame2

			local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

			TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 28)
			TextLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 4)
			TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			TextLabel.Text = "Keybind Editor"
			TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 220)
			TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			TextLabel.TextSize = 14
			TextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			TextLabel.Parent = Frame2

			local TextButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

			TextButton.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
			TextButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 4)
			TextButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			TextButton.Text = "X"
			TextButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
			TextButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
			TextButton.TextSize = 14
			TextButton.Parent = Frame2
			TextButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				ScreenGui:Destroy()
				ScreenGui = nil
			end)

			local TextLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

			TextLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 16)
			TextLabel2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 30)
			TextLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			TextLabel2.Text = "Tap a row → press a key"
			TextLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 160, 160)
			TextLabel2.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
			TextLabel2.TextSize = 10
			TextLabel2.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			TextLabel2.Parent = Frame2

			local ScrollingFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")

			ScrollingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -56)
			ScrollingFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 48)
			ScrollingFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			ScrollingFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
			ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 4
			ScrollingFrame.Parent = Frame2

			local UIListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")

			UIListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
			UIListLayout.Parent = ScrollingFrame

			local t126 = {
				"Flash",
				"Block",
				"Reset",
				"Drop",
				"AutoGrab",
				"Aimbot",
				"Clone",
				"ESPBest",
				"QuickAP",
				"Lagger",
				"BlockPreview",
				"SpamPaintball",
				"AutoTurret",
				"AutoGiant",
				"ResetBalloon",
				"AutoBlock",
				"APESP",
				"StealIntro",
				"AutoReturn",
				"AutoBalloon",
				"BackpackESP"
			}
			local u2764
			local connection7

			for _, v in ipairs(t126) do
				local Frame3 = Instance.new("Frame")

				Frame3.Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0, 28)
				Frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 20, 22)
				Frame3.BorderSizePixel = 0
				Frame3.Parent = ScrollingFrame
				Instance.new("UICorner", Frame3).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

				local TextLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

				TextLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 1, 0)
				TextLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
				TextLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				TextLabel3.Text = t124[v] or v
				TextLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				TextLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
				TextLabel3.TextSize = 11
				TextLabel3.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				TextLabel3.Parent = Frame3

				local TextButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton")

				TextButton2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 22)
				TextButton2.Position = UDim2.new(1, -46, 0.5, -11)
				TextButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 30, 35)
				TextButton2.Text = n175Keybinds[v] or "?"
				TextButton2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 200)
				TextButton2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
				TextButton2.TextSize = 12
				TextButton2.Parent = Frame3
				Instance.new("UICorner", TextButton2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
				TextButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					if connection7 then
						pcall(function()
							connection7:Disconnect()
						end)
						connection7 = nil
					end

					u2764 = v
					TextButton2.Text = "..."
					TextButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 50, 20)
					connection7 = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
						if gameProcessed then
							return
						end

						if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
							return
						end

						if not u2764 then
							return
						end

						local Name = input.KeyCode.Name

						if Name == "Escape" then
							TextButton2.Text = n175Keybinds[u2764] or "?"
							TextButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 30, 35)
							u2764 = nil

							if connection7 then
								connection7:Disconnect()
								connection7 = nil
							end

							return
						end

						local v2772 = u2764

						for k, n175Keybind in pairs(n175Keybinds) do
							if k ~= v2772 and n175Keybind == Name then
								n175Keybinds[k] = n175Keybinds[v2772]
							end
						end

						n175Keybinds[v2772] = Name
						n175SaveKeybinds()
						TextButton2.Text = Name
						TextButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 30, 35)
						u2764 = nil

						if connection7 then
							connection7:Disconnect()
							connection7 = nil
						end

						task.defer(function()
							if ScreenGui then
								ScreenGui:Destroy()
								ScreenGui = nil
							end

							_G._175_OpenKeybindEditor()
						end)
					end)
				end)
			end

			ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #t126 * 32 + 8)
		end
		print("[175] Resize (Win/QAP/Preview) + keybinds OK")
	end)
end

print("175 Flash & Block cargado correctamente!")
